=== PROMPT VARIANT ===
r1

=== PROMPT ===
You will be given a competitive programming problem.
You are given simple undirected graphs G and H, each with N vertices: vertices 1, 2, \ldots, N.
Graph G has M_G edges, and its i-th edge (1\leq i\leq M_G) connects vertices u_i and v_i.
Graph H has M_H edges, and its i-th edge (1\leq i\leq M_H) connects vertices a_i and b_i.
You can perform the following operation on graph H any number of times, possibly zero.

- Choose a pair of integers (i,j) satisfying 1\leq i<j\leq N. Pay A_{i,j} yen, and if there is no edge between vertices i and j in H, add one; if there is, remove it.

Find the minimum total cost required to make G and H isomorphic.
What is a simple undirected graph?
 A simple undirected graph is a graph without self-loops or multi-edges, where edges have no direction.

What does it mean for graphs to be isomorphic?
 Two graphs G and H with N vertices are isomorphic if and only if there exists a permutation (P_1,P_2,\ldots,P_N) of (1,2,\ldots,N) such that for all 1\leq i\lt j\leq N:

-  an edge exists between vertices i and j in G if and only if an edge exists between vertices P_i and P_j in H.

Input

The input is given from Standard Input in the following format:
N
M _ G
u _ 1 v _ 1
u _ 2 v _ 2
\vdots
u _ {M _ G} v _ {M _ G}
M _ H
a _ 1 b _ 1
a _ 2 b _ 2
\vdots
a _ {M _ H} b _ {M _ H}
A _ {1,2} A _ {1,3} \ldots A _ {1,N}
A _ {2,3} \ldots A _ {2,N}
\vdots
A _ {N-1,N}

Output

Print the answer.

Constraints


- 1\leq N\leq8
- 0\leq M _ G\leq\dfrac{N(N-1)}2
- 0\leq M _ H\leq\dfrac{N(N-1)}2
- 1\leq u _ i\lt v _ i\leq N\ (1\leq i\leq M _ G)
- (u _ i,v _ i)\neq(u _ j,v _ j)\ (1\leq i\lt j\leq M _ G)
- 1\leq a _ i\lt b _ i\leq N\ (1\leq i\leq M _ H)
- (a _ i,b _ i)\neq(a _ j,b _ j)\ (1\leq i\lt j\leq M _ H)
- 1\leq A _ {i,j}\leq 10 ^ 6\ (1\leq i\lt j\leq N)
- All input values are integers.

Sample Input 1

5
4
1 2
2 3
3 4
4 5
4
1 2
1 3
1 4
1 5
3 1 4 1
5 9 2
6 5
3

Sample Output 1

9

The given graphs are as follows:

For example, you can perform the following four operations on H to make it isomorphic to G at a cost of 9 yen.

- Choose (i,j)=(1,3). There is an edge between vertices 1 and 3 in H, so pay 1 yen to remove it.
- Choose (i,j)=(2,5). There is no edge between vertices 2 and 5 in H, so pay 2 yen to add it.
- Choose (i,j)=(1,5). There is an edge between vertices 1 and 5 in H, so pay 1 yen to remove it.
- Choose (i,j)=(3,5). There is no edge between vertices 3 and 5 in H, so pay 5 yen to add it.

After these operations, H becomes:

You cannot make G and H isomorphic at a cost less than 9 yen, so print 9.

Reason in a commit-implement-recheck way.

A productive rhythm for this task usually looks like this:
- Isolate the decisive constraints, the target complexity, and at most one or two approaches that are still truly plausible.
- If two candidates remain, compare them only on the decisive constraint and commit quickly to the stronger one instead of keeping a wide search open.
- Before coding, keep only a tiny implementation ledger for the chosen plan: main invariant, key state or transition, and the one or two edge cases most likely to fail.
- Move into code as soon as that ledger is clear. After coding starts, keep reasoning attached to the current draft rather than reopening the high-level search.
- After the first complete draft, do one short implementation-check-adjust-check loop on the same draft: test the key edge case, patch locally if needed, and recheck.
- Reopen the main design only if a concrete counterexample breaks the core invariant. Otherwise prefer the local patch and keep closing.
- Once the current draft, invariant, and short recheck still agree, finalize the solution.

Output only the final Python code enclosed in a code block.

import sys
from itertools import permutations

def main():
    data = list(map(int, sys.stdin.buffer.read().split()))
    it = iter(data)
    N = next(it)
    MG = next(it)
    G = [[False]*N for _ in range(N)]
    for _ in range(MG):
        u = next(it)-1; v = next(it)-1
        G[u][v] = G[v][u] = True
    MH = next(it)
    H = [[False]*N for _ in range(N)]
    for _ in range(MH):
        a = next(it)-1; b = next(it)-1
        H[a][b] = H[b][a] = True
    A = [[0]*N for _ in range(N)]
    for i in range(N-1):
        for j in range(i+1,N):
            val = next(it)
            A[i][j] = A[j][i] = val
    INF = 10**18
    ans = INF
    # maybe precompute edge masks for G and H to speed
    Gmask = [[False]...] no.
    for p in permutations(range(N)):
        cost = 0
        # maybe local variables for speed
        for i in range(N-1):
            pi = p[i]
            Gi = G[i]
            for j in range(i+1,N):
                if Gi[j] != H[pi][p[j]]:
                    cost += A[pi][p[j]]
                    if cost >= ans:
                        # break out
                        pass
        if cost < ans: ans = cost
    print(ans)

```python
import sys
from itertools import permutations

def main():
    data = list(map(int, sys.stdin.buffer.read().split()))
    it = iter(data)
    N = next(it)

    MG = next(it)
    G = [[False] * N for _ in range(N)]
    for _ in range(MG):
        u = next(it) - 1
        v = next(it) - 1
        G[u][v] = G[v][u] = True

    MH = next(it)
    H = [[False] * N for _ in range(N)]
    for _ in range(MH):
        a = next(it) - 1
        b = next(it) - 1
        H[a][b] = H[b][a] = True

    A = [[0] * N for _ in range(N)]
    for i in range(N - 1):
        for j in range(i + 1, N):
            c = next(it)
            A[i][j] = c
            A[j][i] = c

    INF = 10**18
    ans = INF

    for p in permutations(range(N)):
        cost = 0
        for i in range(N - 1):
            pi = p[i]
            Gi = G[i]
            Hi = H[pi]  # Wait, H[pi] is row for H vertex pi. But for pair (pi,pj), H[pi][pj]. Good.
            Ai = A[pi]  # Could use row A[pi][pj].
            for j in range(i + 1, N):
                pj = p[j]
                if Gi[j] != Hi[pj]:
                    cost += Ai[pj]
                    if cost >= ans:
                        # We can break, but not necessary.
                        pass
        if cost < ans:
            ans = cost

    print(ans)

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

was too long and got cut off. Need final code only. Ensure no extraneous text. But final answer should be code block only. Need maybe include code block with code. Let's output concise.

But before final, let's ensure code is correct. Let's maybe write final code now:

```python
import sys
from itertools import permutations

def main():
    it = iter(map(int, sys.stdin.buffer.read().split()))
    N = next(it)

    MG = next(it)
    G = [[False] * N for _ in range(N)]
    for _ in range(MG):
        u = next(it) - 1
        v = next(it) - 1
        G[u][v] = G[v][u] = True

    MH = next(it)
    H = [[False] * N for _ in range(N)]
    for _ in range(MH):
        a = next(it) - 1
        b = next(it) - 1
        H[a][b] = H[b][a] = True

    A = [[0] * N for _ in range(N)]
    for i in range(N - 1):
        for j in range(i + 1, N):
            c = next(it)
            A[i][j] = c
            A[j][i] = c

    INF = 10**18
    ans = INF

    for p in permutations(range(N)):
        cost = 0
        for i in range(N - 1):
            pi = p[i]
            Gi = G[i]
            Hi = H[pi]
            Ai = A[pi]
            for j in range(i + 1, N):
                pj = p[j]
                if Gi[j] != Hi[pj]:
                    cost += Ai[pj]
        if cost < ans:
            ans = cost

    print(ans)

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

Need check if `Hi = H[pi]` and `Ai = A[pi]` inside i loop. If pi is H vertex, H[pi] row. Since H symmetric, H[pi][pj] correct. A[pi][pj] correct. Good.

Potential issue: If `cost` exceeds ans, no break; okay. But if ans is INF, cost int. Good.

Now, final answer code block only.</think>```python
import sys
from itertools import permutations

def main():
    it = iter(map(int, sys.stdin.buffer.read().split()))
    N = next(it)

    MG = next(it)
    G = [[False] * N for _ in range(N)]
    for _ in range(MG):
        u = next(it) - 1
        v = next(it) - 1
        G[u][v] = G[v][u] = True

    MH = next(it)
    H = [[False] * N for _ in range(N)]
    for _ in range(MH):
        a = next(it) - 1
        b = next(it) - 1
        H[a][b] = H[b][a] = True

    A = [[0] * N for _ in range(N)]
    for i in range(N - 1):
        for j in range(i + 1, N):
            c = next(it)
            A[i][j] = c
            A[j][i] = c

    INF = 10**18
    ans = INF

    for p in permutations(range(N)):
        cost = 0
        for i in range(N - 1):
            pi = p[i]
            Gi = G[i]
            Hi = H[pi]
            Ai = A[pi]
            for j in range(i + 1, N):
                pj = p[j]
                if Gi[j] != Hi[pj]:
                    cost += Ai[pj]
        if cost < ans:
            ans = cost

    print(ans)

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```
